# Electron2D declarations without an audited upstream row

Last updated: 2026-10-03

These declarations are present in the compiled runtime. A blank upstream cell means no exact counterpart was established by the conservative name-and-arity mapper; it does not claim an intentional extension. Review each against the linked Godot class page and record a rationale before declaring parity.

| Godot API | Electron2D API | State | Reason / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| — | [`public struct Electron2D.AnimationAudioKey`](../classes/AnimationAudioKey.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: ADR 0093 typed audio key value/borrowed resource/trim container or exact add_track(TYPE_AUDIO) node-role factory projection. ADR 0093/0047 prepared audio-track execution. AnimationAudioTrackTests verifies typed keys/copies/trims, native stream/sample PCM, startup/elapsed seek/update-only, receiver pause/controller stop/cache cleanup, end trim/zero capacity, randomizer/graph and 64 warmed repeated cue starts with zero owner managed bytes and no engine native allocation-call growth. Two Linux Wayland GPU and two compatibility emitter hosts check native PCM/control/cleanup and actual canvas output. SDL dummy is transport capture, not physical listening; native library/driver internals, other platforms, editor/disk and human acceptance remain unverified. (class reference). |
| — | [`public struct Electron2D.AnimationBezierKey`](../classes/AnimationBezierKey.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: ADR 0093 exact typed projection of method key/argument identity, scalar Bézier key geometry, typed track insertion/key replacement or explicit callback cache preparation. No universal payload/property container or reflection invocation. ADR 0093 typed method/Bézier execution. AnimationSpecialTrackTests verifies scalar cubic time/value handles, typed mixed-signature callback keys/copies, forward/reverse/sections/seeks/loops, graph path filters and weighted curves, deferred/immediate mutation/failure/disposal, prepared capacity exhaustion/reuse and 256 warmed scalar and deferred frames with zero managed bytes. Two Linux Wayland GPU and two compatibility hosts verify five actual curve/color pixel poses with borrowed-resource cleanup. Native/driver allocations, other platforms, disk/editor round trips and human acceptance remain unverified. (class reference). |

| — | [`public enum Electron2D.PacketReadStatus`](../classes/PacketReadStatus.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: Typed constructor, owner/lifetime/I/O hook, caller-span overload, failure diagnostic or explicit decoder budget for native byte transports. ADR 0094: executable typed streams and native TCP/UDP/UDS listeners/peers plus fixed little-endian packet framing. .NET socket backend, owner-thread operations/disposal, span progress or typed I/O exceptions replace heterogeneous Error/Array results; exact-width integer/IEEE codecs, ASCII spaces and UTF-8 BOM/NUL/malformed-byte behavior preserve wire semantics. Native Linux x64 NetworkingTests verifies IPv4/IPv6 loopback, IPC cleanup, port conflict/restart, partial transfer/FIN, datagram/framing boundaries and budgets, connected filtering, UDP endpoint/lifetime/limit routing, multicast membership, scene-owned request/reply and 64 warm buffer/TCP/UDP managed-allocation boundaries. Browser raw sockets, other platforms, routed network/multicast/broadcast, native allocator totals and owner acceptance remain separate gates. (class reference). |

| — | [`public LineEdit()`](../classes/LineEdit.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: Typed constructor, scene/lifecycle/input/resource hook or permanent text-action configuration for the executable single-line editor. Executable LineEdit editing/display slice under ADRs 0008/0038/0046/0051/0083: scalar text limits, complete shaped-line caret and visual selection geometry, grapheme and word movement, horizontal scrolling, password/placeholder display, focus/edit state, native committed/preedit input, selection/clipboard, undo/redo, Unicode-control commands, typed packing, themed backgrounds/focus/outline/icons and text drag/drop. Pinned line_edit.cpp/text_server.cpp audit. LineEditTests verifies boundary/rejection/history/storage/action/IME/drag/failure paths, native X11 Linux GPU/compatibility pixels and input, clipboard restoration and 64 warmed caret/selection render-mutation intervals with zero managed bytes. TextChangeRejected and history notifications see committed state before observers; native InputEvent resource copies and text-shaping mutations are outside that warm measurement. Native allocator totals, cross-platform text/virtual keyboard and owner acceptance remain unverified. (class reference). |

| — | [`public static readonly Electron2D.AnimationParameter<Electron2D.AnimationNodeStateMachinePlayback> Playback`](../classes/AnimationNodeStateMachine.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: ADR 0093 exact typed playback parameter, C# resource constructor/lifecycle or immutable schema hook projection. AnimationStateMachineTests checks authoring/defaults/guards/aliases, geometric priority routes and teleport/group-exit fallback, immediate/sync/end/loop/curve/boundary fades, typed flags/predicates, automatic cycles/priorities, multiple groups/events, independent shared trees, rename/removal, preparation/observer failure, reentry/test-only and method/nested effects. Native SDL-dummy FAudio PCM checks cue start/restart/stop and departed nonblended output; two GPU and two compatibility Wayland hosts verify six actual pixel poses and borrowed-resource cleanup. ADR 0093 executable typed state machines. AnimationStateMachineTests checks authoring/defaults/guards/aliases, geometric priority routes and teleport/group-exit fallback, immediate/sync/end/loop/curve/boundary fades, typed flags/predicates, automatic cycles/priorities, multiple groups/events, independent shared trees, rename/removal, preparation/observer failure, reentry/test-only and method/nested effects. Warm 64 command/route cycles, 64 cached grouped starts and 64 idle frames allocate zero owner managed bytes. Native SDL-dummy FAudio PCM checks cue start/restart/stop and departed nonblended output; two GPU and two compatibility Wayland hosts verify six actual pixel poses and borrowed-resource cleanup. Native allocator internals, physical listening, other platforms, disk/editor and human acceptance remain unverified. (class reference). |

| — | [`protected override System.Func<Electron2D.Node> CreateSceneInstanceFactory()`](../classes/BackBufferCopy.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: Typed constructor/scene factory, stored descriptor or configuration-warning override for native canvas composition. ADRs 0008/0014/0028 executable native group/screen composition: same-Z child grouping, fitted/default or recorded owner geometry, once-only SelfModulate opacity, fit/clear margins, native backbuffer copies at draw order, implicit first screen snapshot, and reserved SCREEN_TEXTURE/SCREEN_PIXEL_SIZE in shared SPIR-V. CanvasCompositionTests exercises typed packing/guards/callback failures, GPU/hardware compatibility pixels, clipped/disabled/hidden/masked/Z copies, group transforms/Y-sort/recorded masks/empty groups, SubViewport alpha/resize and 64 active/64 idle stable-size frames with zero managed bytes. GPU HLSL/GLSL exercise custom group materials, clear margins and generated explicit LOD. Compatibility rejects custom shaders/mipmaps; software rejects premultiplied group composition before drawing. Nested same-Z groups and writable-backbuffer sampling reject explicitly; inherited alpha-mask modes now execute through CanvasItem.ClipChildren; nested mask/group ranges explicitly reject. Editor inspector hiding, other platforms, native allocator totals and owner acceptance remain unverified or dependent. (class reference). |

| — | [`public override System.String[] GetConfigurationWarnings()`](../classes/CanvasItem.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: Pinned canvas_item.cpp/.h, renderer_canvas_cull.cpp and renderer_canvas_render_rd.cpp audit: stored Disabled/Only/AndDraw with Max sentinel, equal-write silence and commit-before-warning refresh; drawn command/texture/tint alpha masks same-Z descendants over the copied background. AndDraw inserts ordinary owner drawing before children and always uses built-in final masking; Only accepts an overriding material. No recorded commands or no captured child range preserve ordinary drawing. CanvasGroup stores policy without replacing its group mode. Physical-ancestor clipping/group warnings preserve the source boundary; nested same-Z masks/groups and writable screen reads explicitly reject under ADR 0028. CanvasClipTests verifies guards, callbacks, packing, native Wayland GPU/hardware compatibility pixels, texture/partial/overlap alpha, Y-sort, Z/TopLevel/neutral boundaries, transparent SubViewport/resize, custom HLSL/GLSL Only materials, and 64 active plus 64 idle warmed frames with zero managed bytes. Software advanced mask blending rejects before drawing. Native/driver allocations, other platforms, editor inspector hiding and owner visual acceptance remain separate gates. (class reference). |

| — | [`protected override System.Func<Electron2D.Node> CreateSceneInstanceFactory()`](../classes/LineEdit.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: Typed constructor, scene/lifecycle/input/resource hook or permanent text-action configuration for the executable single-line editor. Executable LineEdit editing/display slice under ADRs 0008/0038/0046/0051/0083: scalar text limits, complete shaped-line caret and visual selection geometry, grapheme and word movement, horizontal scrolling, password/placeholder display, focus/edit state, native committed/preedit input, selection/clipboard, undo/redo, Unicode-control commands, typed packing, themed backgrounds/focus/outline/icons and text drag/drop. Pinned line_edit.cpp/text_server.cpp audit. LineEditTests verifies boundary/rejection/history/storage/action/IME/drag/failure paths, native X11 Linux GPU/compatibility pixels and input, clipboard restoration and 64 warmed caret/selection render-mutation intervals with zero managed bytes. TextChangeRejected and history notifications see committed state before observers; native InputEvent resource copies and text-shaping mutations are outside that warm measurement. Native allocator totals, cross-platform text/virtual keyboard and owner acceptance remain unverified. (class reference). |

| — | [`protected System.Void CheckPacketPeer()`](../classes/PacketPeer.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: Typed constructor, owner/lifetime/I/O hook, caller-span overload, failure diagnostic or explicit decoder budget for native byte transports. ADR 0094: executable typed streams and native TCP/UDP/UDS listeners/peers plus fixed little-endian packet framing. .NET socket backend, owner-thread operations/disposal, span progress or typed I/O exceptions replace heterogeneous Error/Array results; exact-width integer/IEEE codecs, ASCII spaces and UTF-8 BOM/NUL/malformed-byte behavior preserve wire semantics. File/editor authoring, multiview, independent native subwindows, native allocator totals, other platforms and owner acceptance remain separate prerequisites/gates. (class reference). |

| — | [`protected override System.Void CopyCustomStateTo(Electron2D.Resource target, System.Boolean deep, Electron2D.DeepDuplicateMode subresourceMode, System.Func<Electron2D.Resource, Electron2D.Resource> duplicateSubresource, System.Func<Electron2D.Resource, Electron2D.Resource> forceDuplicateSubresource)`](../classes/X509Certificate.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: ADR 0094: typed constructor, resource-copy/reset/disposal/finalization hook or shared TLS phase for executable TLS/security resources. ADR 0094 TLS delivery: copied BCL PEM/DER key/ordered certificate-chain resources, typed weak loader cache, immutable borrowed TLS options, private Linux system OpenSSL 3 TLS 1.2/1.3 over any StreamPeer with prepared 64 KiB BIO pair and caller spans. TLSTests checks native client/server handshake, fragmented/128 KiB data, chain/expiry/DNS/IP/override/unsafe policies, independent SslStream interoperability both roles, close-notify/FIN/reuse, malformed input/identity rollback, key/cert retention, copies/cache/observer failures/finalization, owner rejection and 64 active/idle zero-managed-allocation polling cycles. Typed exceptions replace Error codes; mismatch status is assigned only to actual hostname/IP mismatch. Other platform packaging/backends, native OpenSSL allocator totals, routed throughput and owner acceptance remain separate gates. (class reference). |

| — | [`public System.Exception LastReadException { get;  }`](../classes/PacketPeer.md) | Implemented | Electron2D-specific: Typed constructor, owner/lifetime/I/O hook, caller-span overload, failure diagnostic or explicit decoder budget for native byte transports. ADR 0094: executable typed streams and native TCP/UDP/UDS listeners/peers plus fixed little-endian packet framing. .NET socket backend, owner-thread operations/disposal, span progress or typed I/O exceptions replace heterogeneous Error/Array results; exact-width integer/IEEE codecs, ASCII spaces and UTF-8 BOM/NUL/malformed-byte behavior preserve wire semantics. Native Linux x64 NetworkingTests verifies IPv4/IPv6 loopback, IPC cleanup, port conflict/restart, partial transfer/FIN, datagram/framing boundaries and budgets, connected filtering, UDP endpoint/lifetime/limit routing, multicast membership, scene-owned request/reply and 64 warm buffer/TCP/UDP managed-allocation boundaries. Browser raw sockets, other platforms, routed network/multicast/broadcast, native allocator totals and owner acceptance remain separate gates. (class reference). |
